Code: NEW src/core/touchZones.js (pure, DOM-free: ZONE + zoneFor(x,w) midline->RIGHT + thrustFromDrag(dx,dy,thresh,maxPx) -> {x,y,mag} (|d|<=thresh -> 0 boundary-inclusive; beyond: direction preserved, mag linear 0->1 across [thresh,maxPx], clamps 1)) + src/render/input.js REWRITTEN dual-zone (keyboard + mouse paths byte-identical to ST-033; LEFT = floating thrust stick (origin = down-point, DEADZONE 24 px, MAX_PX 90 px linear ramp, auto-centre on release, NEVER fires, NEVER writes pointer.ndc) / RIGHT = aim (ndc via aimFromPoint on down + move) + fire while down + tap tail; 2-finger pause = same-zone (PAUSE_COOLDOWN 0.45 s + onPauseConfirm); cross-zone = thrust + fire no-pause; 3rd+ fingers ignored; duplicate same-id pointerdown no-op; onBlur clears all; new getters touchDrag (null in deadzone) + rightAimActive) + src/main.js (createInput {onPause: togglePause, onPauseConfirm: toast}; resolveAimPoint priority rightAimActive (raycast ndc) -> touchDrag (aim along the drag, ST-033 carryover) -> pointer.active raycast; + window.__vr.input probe handle) + UI copy (tutorialHint "LEFT: DRAG TO FLY · RIGHT: AIM + FIRE · 2-FINGER PAUSE" + screens.js/tutorialOverlay.js control rows LEFT ZONE/RIGHT ZONE/2 FINGERS). (2) applySettings is a FULL-OBJECT replace: applySettings({fx:'off'}) alone wipes volume/sfx -> audio.setVolume(NaN) throws; pass {...storage.getSettings(), fx} or use the F hotkey; recovered via applySettings(storage.getSettings()). INCIDENT (carried from I268, FIXED): writeBOM latin1 bug - Buffer.from(String.fromCharCode(0xFEFF)) encodes the BOM as ONE 0xFF byte, not EF BB BF; the fixed helper writes BOMs as raw [0xEF,0xBB,0xBF] prefixes + latin1 content (stray 0xFF stripped from all 5 files; I268 commit amended to 178c58b). Bounded YouTube MCP research: 1 search / 3 queries / 24 raw, 2 transcript pulls (new burned ids: HhCRAJIUq6E, I-DNl36mNqw, ZoL6Em5OZl4) - grounding: (a) the galactic band is a STRAIGHT BAND with HEAVY CENTRAL CONCENTRATION - one dominant off-center knot, not uniform; (b) layering = dense thin-disk stripe + puffier faint thick-disk envelope + bulge + halo; (c) dust = dark constellations / Great-Rift-style rifts occluding the core (the center reads as a dark abyss); (d) gas + dust -> star formation -> star clusters correlate with the bright knots. Art direction appended to production/scope-check-E40.md as "## Art Direction (ST-331, I363 - grounded)". KEEP: equirect 1024x512 JPEG; band elevation (e=0 plane, k=100, boost 0.5 scaffold; I360 MENU strip y 180-240 x 420-860 still lands); warm amber R>G>B band + dark navy 0x04060f field; band base brightness A/B vs installed v5 (p50 within +/-5 luma, D8). ADD: (1) dominant core knot - azimuth ~65% map width (free param, D4), radius 8-10 deg, +35-45 luma, luma < 245, partially veiled glow x (1 - riftMask*0.7) (D1); (2) 2-3 secondary knots, radius 3-5 deg, +15-25 luma, one may be slightly cooler warm-white; (3) layered band - core stripe + wider envelope (2.5x width, ~40% amp) + faint far companion (~15% amp, 4x width, e +4-6 deg, slightly cooler, D5); (4) dust - 1 dominant broad rift near the core knot (darkening 0.35-0.55, 25-35% band width, soft noise-displaced edges) + 2-4 minor lanes (0.25-0.45); (5) fBm filaments - 3-4 octaves, domain-warped, anisotropic 3-4x along-band stretch, asymmetric wisps; (6) sparse correlated stars - density 0.4*base + 1.2*bandBrightness + 1.6*knotProximity, 1-2 px Gaussian dots, warm-white majority + ~12-15% cool-white, few 2-3 px brights in knots only (D2: dense twinkling stays in the in-game Points layer). AVOID: luma >= 245; elevation/width change (I360 framing invariant); cool drift in the band; high-freq 1 px dust edges; uniform fine noise (bake 2x + downsample 4x4, D3). Palette ramp (D6): deep amber (R 120-150, G 70-90, B 40-55) -> bright amber-white (R 235, G 200, B 150) at peaks; field (4-8, 6-10, 15-25); R>G>B everywhere in band. Size strategy: bake 2048x1024 linear (tree.update_tag commit) -> downsample 1024x512 4x4 -> JPEG q90 -> if > 115 KB then q85 -> then reduce far-band then filament amplitude (never core structure); hard stop 122880 B (D7 - the tests/skyboxTexture.test.js cap, tighter than the budget-derived 123557 B); target 90-115 KB. Decisions D1-D8 recorded (D1 core veiled by rift [real look + anti-blowout]; D2 sparse skybox stars; D3 bake 2x downsample; D4 knot azimuth free / elevation invariant; D5 far band ~15% at +4-6 deg; D6 palette ramp; D7 cap 122880 B; D8 band brightness A/B vs v5). I230 Blender 5.2 gotchas re-listed for ST-332: math-only unit direction (VectorMath LENGTH/NORMALIZE broken), Math EXPONENT = 2^x, Noise node has no vector offset (per-channel ADD on U), bake-target node select must commit tree.update_tag(), 8-bit PNG writes stored bytes as-is (I357 linear-as-bytes = ~6x too dark), 8-bit R<<G green cast (warm R>G>B avoids), dense 1 px stars kill PNG compression.
